Binary search:
 def proj(h):
  low = h.min() - 1.0
  high = h.max()
  # f(tau)=sum(clip(h-tau,0,1)) decreasing in tau
  for 60:
    mid=(low+high)/2; val=...; if val>target: low=mid else high=mid
  tau=(low+high)/2; z=clip(h-tau,0,1);
  # adjust finite rounding: diff=target-z.sum(); distribute over free variables (0<z<1)
  free = (z>1e-12)&(z<1-1e-12); if diff and count: z[free]+=diff/count; else distribute to non-bound.
  return clip.

Projection: Need fast. Binary search 50 iterations. Could use adaptive initial. Implement:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2
    y=np.asarray(h,float)
    y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    # if sum close return with adjustment
    # binary search tau
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max())
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       z=np.clip(y-mid,0,1)
       s=z.sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
    z=np.clip(y-tau,0,1)
    diff=target-z.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       free=(z>1e-10)&(z<1-1e-10)
       if free.any(): z[free] += diff / free.sum()
       else:
          # if all zero/one, adjust a bounded variable? find one with 0<target count? target integer; sum diff likely floating small
          # distribute among variables with z>0 or <1
          candidates=np.where((z==0)|(z==1))[0]
          # adjust those away from bound by diff, then clip
          z[candidates[:n]] += diff/n
          z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    return z
```
If no free and diff not small, this may break target. But if h already {0,1} and target integer, diff should be small. We can use exact integer adjustment: if diff positive, find zeros and set one to 1? But gradient continuous. We'll do robust:
- If all at bounds, sum(z) should be integer count ones. target integer if n even. diff due floating; to exact target, adjust one free zero? If diff>0 (need more mass), choose one z<1 (zero) and add min(diff,1); if diff >1 not, but sum target integer and diff small. If diff<0 choose one z>0 subtract. This changes integer but okay. We can loop.
Simpler: after binary, if diff>1e-8 and no free, use indices sorted by closeness to 0.5? But none.

def upsample(h,n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new)
    g=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
    return g
```

Could implement binary objective fast:
```
def binary_c_counts(B,n):
  maxcnt=0
  mask_all=(1<<n)-1
  for d in range(1,n):
    shifted = (B >> d) & mask_all
    maskd=(1<<(n-d))-1
    cnt10 = (B & ~shifted & maskd).bit_count()
    cnt01 = (shifted & ~B & maskd).bit_count()
    maxcnt=max(maxcnt,cnt10,cnt01)
  return maxcnt*2.0/n
```
But for n=1000, maskd=(1<<(n-d))-1 each loop creates big int; can precompute masks? list of n big ints length1000 of 1000 bits ~0.125MB each? Actually 1000*125B=125KB? def binary_sa(B,n,time_budget,seed):
  current=init; curr_c=binary_c(B,n)
  best=...
  temp=0.05 maybe in C units.
  while time:
    choose random 1 index a and 0 index b (using lists or random trials)
    swap bits: B2 = B ^ (1<<a) ^ (1<<b)
    c2=binary_c(B2,n)
    if c2 <= curr_c or rng.random() < exp((curr_c-c2)/T): accept
    if c2<best: update
    cool T slowly.
    periodic local improvement: for best promising? 
```

2. `compute_c_lags(h,n)`:
```
def compute_c_lags(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    total=float(h.sum())
    pref=np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0; pref[1:]=np.cumsum(h)
    f=np.fft.rfft(h, 2*n)
    q=np.fft.irfft(f * np.conjugate(f), 2*n)[:n]
    c=np.empty(2*n-1)
    mid=n-1
    c[mid]=dx*(total-q[0])
    for e in range(1,n):
       c[mid+e]=dx*(pref[n-e]-q[e])
       c[mid-e]=dx*(total-pref[e]-q[e])
    return c
```
This loop in Python over n e. For n=1000, 1000 loops per eval, maybe overhead. We can vectorize! Yes.
For e=1..n-1: `ee=np.arange(1,n)`, `m=n-ee`, `idx_pos=mid+ee`, `idx_neg=mid-ee`, `c[idx_pos]=dx*(pref[m]-q[ee])`, `c[idx_neg]=dx*(total-pref[ee]-q[ee])`. Good.
```
ee=np.arange(1,n)
c[mid+ee] = dx*(pref[n-ee] - q[ee])
c[mid-ee] = dx*(total - pref[ee] - q[ee])
```
This is vectorized O(n). Great.
Need allocate q length n. FFT. For n maybe small, okay.

`compute_c_lags_max` just `compute_c_lags(h,n).max()`.

6. Objective functions:
```
def soft_active_obj(x,n,active,alpha,mu):
    c=compute_c_lags(x,n)
    vals=c[active]
    m=vals.max()
    sh=alpha*(vals-m)
    sh-=sh.max()
    val=m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))/alpha
    val += mu*(float(x.sum()-0.5*n))**2
    return float(val)
```

9. Projected gradient driver:
```
def run_pg(h,n,deadline, n_iter=200):
  h=project; best_exact=exact; best_est=...
  active_count=...
  alphas=[50,200,1000]
  for alpha in alphas:
    for it in range(n_iter):
      if time>deadline: break
      c=compute; active=select
      vals=c[active]; m=vals.max(); sh=alpha*(vals-m); sh-=max; w=...
      g=active_grad with w
      g -= g.mean()
      norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if norm<1e-10: break
      g/=norm
      best_t=0; best_m= c.max()
      # steps: start maybe 0.1, down to 1e-4
      for t in [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]:
        h2=project(h+t*g,n)
        e2=compute_c_lags_max(h2,n)
        if e2 < best_m - 1e-12:
           best_m=e2; best_t=t
      if best_t==0:
         # try smaller or break
         break
      h2=project(h+best_t*g,n)
      est=best_m
      if est < best_est - 1e-10: update exact periodically
    # after alpha
```
This uses unit gradient, step sizes up to 0.05; h values can move significantly. Maybe okay. Could use adaptive t0 based on 1/norm? But we normalized.
Use c_lags max estimate, not exact. Exact every maybe when est improvement >1e-5.

def binary_sa(h,n,deadline,seed):
  if n%2 !=0 or n==0: return None
  B_init=from h; curr_count=...
  best...

  T=20.0; minT=0.2; cool factor.
  while time<deadline:
    # cooling: T=max(minT, T*0.9995)
    while inner? one swap per eval
    a=rng.integers(0,n); b=rng.integers(0,n)

def improve_one(h,n,deadline):
    h=project; best...
    # estimate time
    t_est = estimate_eval_time(h,n, min(10,2n-1))
    # L-BFGS until deadline - reserve
    reserve = min(80, (deadline - time.time())*0.25)
    lbfgs_deadline = deadline - max(30,reserve)
    run_lbfgs(h,n,lbfgs_deadline, eval_time=t_est, max_active=...)
    best updated
    if time<deadline-10:
       run_slsqp(best,n,deadline-10, active)
    if time<deadline-5:
       run_pg(best,n,deadline-5)
    return project(best), exact(best)
```

Let's write `run_lbfgs` in detail:
```
def run_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, eval_time=0.01, max_active=None):
    if max_active is None: max_active = int(min(24, max(6, 6 + n//200)))
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h; best_c=exact_c5(h,n); best_est=compute_c_lags_max(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    mu=1e-2
    # choose alpha schedule: maybe nested
    alpha_schedule = [20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1200.0, 4000.0, 12000.0]
    # rounds: each alpha maybe one minimize. Also mean at start.
    # For each, time_slice based on remaining and eval_time.
    # maxiter = int(min(400, max(20, time_slice / (6*eval_time))))
    do_mean=True
    for alpha in [None] + alpha_schedule:
       if time.time()>deadline-3: break
       c=compute_c_lags(h,n)
       active=select_active(c,max_active, rel_gap=0.002, abs_gap=2e-4)
       remaining=deadline-time.time()
       if remaining < 10: break
       # each minimize slice up to min(60, remaining/2)
       slice_time = min(60.0, remaining*0.35)
       if alpha is None: obj=mean_active_obj; jac=mean_active_grad; args=(n,active,mu); maxiter=int(...)
       else: obj=soft_active_obj; jac=soft_active_grad; args=(n,active,alpha,mu)
       opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-20,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':max(iter*5,50),'maxls':30}
       try: res=minimize(obj,h,jac=jac,args=args,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options=opts)
       except Exception: res=None
       if res is not None:
          h2=project_feasible(res.x,n)
          est=compute_c_lags_max(h2,n)
          if est < best_est - 1e-12:
             exc=exact_c5(h2,n)
             if exc < best_c:
                best_h=h2; best_c=exc; best_est=est
             h=h2
          else:
             # maybe exact could be better? skip
             h=h2  # continue from projected even if not exact lower? Could help
       # If res.x has NaN, reset?
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): h=best_h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c,best_est
```
After each minimize, even if exact not improve, set h=h2 to continue? If h2 estimated not lower but close, may not help; but could. Use if est <= best_est +1e-8. Also project. If exact not update, best remains.
Maybe not set h to h2 if est worse, to avoid drift. Use h=h2 only if est < best_est or est <= best_est +1e-10. Actually L-BFGS from previous h, res.x likely optimized the non-exact active objective; even if full est not lower, may be good. Set h=project(res.x) if finite.

But if res doesn't improve and best_est not lower, repeating same active may stall. Outer alpha changes.

Could use `best_h` as starting point for each alpha? If using res.x, may drift. Maybe start each alpha from best_h to avoid worsening. But local basin. Use h_cur separately: after an alpha if cur_est < best_est +small, set h_cur else revert best. We'll maintain `h_cur` and `best_h`.
```

...

est=...

    cur_h=hproj; cur_est=est

    exact update best
```

def binary_max_count_limit(B,n,masks,limit):
    maxc=0
    # Optional if limit==0 return 0? no
    for d, mask in masks:
        if maxc >= limit:
            return limit
        s=(B >> d) & mask
        t = ((B ^ s) & mask).bit_count()
        if t + maxc? Not enough for orientation. Need compute t10 maybe.
        t10 = (B & ~s & mask).bit_count()

        # t01 = t - t10

        if t01 > maxc:
            maxc=t01
            if maxc >= limit: return limit
    return maxc

Bits to B efficiently:
```
def bits_to_B(bits):
    B=0
    # loop over enumerate
    for i,b in enumerate(bits):
       if b: B |= 1 << i
    return B
```

B to bits:
```
def B_to_bits(B,n):
    out=np.empty(n,float)
    for i in range(n): out[i]=((B>>i)&1)
    return out
```

ones=np.where(bits==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(bits==0)[0]
target=n//2
diff=target - len(ones)
if diff>0: # flip zeros to ones, choose zeros with largest h
   order=zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])][:diff]

Detailed function implementations:

`project_feasible(h,n)`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n == 0:
        return y
    # if sum exactly close and bounds, adjust slightly
    s = float(y.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
        # ensure exact within float maybe
        diff = target - s
        if diff != 0.0:
            free = np.where((y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
            if free.size:
                y[free[0]] += diff
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
        return y
    # binary search threshold
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max())
    # It is possible h all one/zero; target within [0,n].
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        z = np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(z.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    y = np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 0:
        free = np.where((y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if free.size:
            y[free] += diff / float(free.size)
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - float(y.sum())
            if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
                # adjust one of the free (or any) by diff
                if free.size:
                    y[free[0]] += diff
                    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
                else:
                    y[0] += diff
                    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            # all at bounds, target integer if n even; adjust one if possible
            if diff > 0:
                idxs = np.where(y < 0.5)[0]
            else:
                idxs = np.where(y > 0.5)[0]
            if idxs.size:
                idx = idxs[0]
                y[idx] += diff
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
            else:
                y[0] += diff
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final tiny correction if possible
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 0:
        free = np.where((y > 1e-9) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-9))[0]
        if free.size:
            y[free[0]] += diff
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y
```
This may call np.clip multiple. Good.

Could there be target not integer and all at bounds impossible; but n even. OK.

`compute_c_lags`:
```
def compute_c_lags(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    total=float(h.sum())
    pref=np.empty(n+1,dtype=float)
    pref[0]=0.0
    np.cumsum(h,out=pref[1:])
    f=np.fft.rfft(h, 2*n)
    q=np.fft.irfft(f * np.conjugate(f), 2*n)[:n]
    c=np.empty(2*n-1,dtype=float)
    mid=n-1
    c[mid]=dx*(total-q[0])
    if n>1:
        ee=np.arange(1,n,dtype=int)
        m=n-ee
        c[mid+ee]=dx*(pref[m]-q[ee])
        c[mid-ee]=dx*(total-pref[ee]-q[ee])
    return c
```
Need ensure `pref[m]` where m array int, works. q[ee].
If q has tiny imaginary? irfft returns real.

`compute_c_lags_max` returns float(c.max()).
Potential q[0] small negative? c0 could slightly off; exact final. OK.

`select_active`:
```
def select_active(c, max_active=10, rel_gap=0.002, abs_gap=2e-4):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    gap=max(abs_gap, rel_gap*m)
    cand=np.where(c >= m - gap)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
        cand=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    min_active=3
    if cand.size < min_active:
        top=np.argsort(c)[-min(min_active, 2*len(c)):]
        add=[idx for idx in top if idx not in cand]? Need set for efficiency.
```
No lambdas. Use sets? Could use boolean mask.
```
    if cand.size > max_active:
        order=np.argsort(c[cand])[::-1][:max_active]
        cand=cand[order]
    else:
        if cand.size < min_active:
            top=np.argsort(c)[-min( min_active, len(c)):]
            inmask=np.zeros(len(c), dtype=bool)
            inmask[cand]=True
            add=top[~inmask[top]]
            if add.size:
                # ensure total max_active
                room = max_active - cand.size
                if add.size > room: add=add[:room]
                cand=np.concatenate((cand, add))
    return cand.astype(int, copy=False)
```
Need if len(c)<=min_active, top all. Good.
Return maybe unsorted; fine.

`add_lag_grad`:
```
def add_lag_grad(g, x, n, idx, scale):
    mid=n-1
    d=int(idx)-mid
    if d == 0:
       if scale !=0:
          g += scale*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
       return
    e=abs(d)
    m=n-e
    if m <=0: return
    if d > 0:
       g[:m] += scale*(1.0 - x[e:n])
       if e !=0:
          g[e:] += scale*(-x[:m])
    else:
       g[e:] += scale*(1.0 - x[:m])
       if e !=0:
          g[:m] += scale*(-x[e:n])
```
For e>0, e!=0. x[e:n] length n-e=m, x[:m]. Good. If scale maybe array? weight float.

`active_grad(x,n,active,weights)`:
```
g=np.zeros(n,dtype=float); dx=2.0/n
for idx,wt in zip(active,weights):
   add_lag_grad(g,x,n,idx,float(wt)*dx)
return g
```
If weights None uniform; we can handle in driver.

Objective functions:
```
def soft_active_obj(x,n,active,alpha,mu):
    c=compute_c_lags(x,n)
    vals=c[active]
    if vals.size == 0: return 0.0? no
    m=float(vals.max())
    sh=alpha*(vals-m)
    sh=sh-sh.max()
    val=m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh))))/alpha
    if mu !=0:
       diff=float(x.sum()-0.5*n)
       val += mu*diff*diff
    return float(val)
```
If active maybe empty? no.
Mean similar. `mean_active_obj(x,n,active,mu)`.
Gradient soft:
```
def soft_active_grad(x,n,active,alpha,mu):
   c=compute_c_lags; vals; m; sh; sh-=max; w=exp; sumw=w.sum(); w/sumw
   g=zeros; dx=2/n
   for idx,wt zip: add...
   if mu: diff=sum-target; g += 2*mu*diff
   return g
```
Mean grad weights=1/len.
Need if `sumw==0` (should not), weights set uniform.

`estimate_eval_time(h,n,active_count)`:
```
def estimate_eval_time(h,n,active_count=10):
    active=np.arange(min(active_count, 2*n-1))
    reps=3
    t=time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(reps):
       soft_active_obj(h,n,active,100.0,0.0)
       soft_active_grad(h,n,active,100.0,0.0)
    dt=(time.perf_counter()-t)/float(reps)
    return max(1e-4, dt)
```
For n maybe large, active indices 0..9 may correspond to negative lags; fine. If 2n-1<10. Good.

`run_lbfgs`: Need update best exact. Use local `best_h`, `best_c`, `best_est`.
```
def run_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,eval_time=None,max_active=None):
    t_start=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n); best_est=compute_c_lags_max(h,n)
    if eval_time is None: eval_time=estimate_eval_time(h,n,10)
    if max_active is None: max_active=int(min(20, max(6, 4 + n//200)))
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    mu=1e-2
    alphas=[10.0,60.0,250.0,1000.0,4000.0,15000.0]
    # mean first
   chedules???
    tasks = [('mean', None)] + [('soft', a) for a in alphas]
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_est=best_est
    # To avoid too many minizes if time short, use time slice dynamic.
    for name, alpha in tasks:
       if time.time() > deadline - 3: break
       c=compute_c_lags(cur_h,n)
       active=select_active(c,max_active)
       remaining=deadline-time.time()
       if remaining < 8: break
       slice_time=min(45.0, max(5.0, remaining*0.30))
       maxiter=int(min(350, max(20, slice_time / max(8.0*eval_time,1e-4))))
       maxfun=max(50, 6*maxiter)
       opts={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-18, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxls': 30, 'm': 20}
       if name=='mean':
           res_type = minimize(mean_active_obj, cur_h, jac=mean_active_grad, args=(n,active,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       else:
           res_type = minimize(soft_active_obj, cur_h, jac=soft_active_grad, args=(n,active,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       if res_type is not None and hasattr(res_type,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res_type.x)):
           candidate=project_feasible(res_type.x,n)
           est=compute_c_lags_max(candidate,n)
           if est <= cur_est + 1e-10:
               cur_h=candidate; cur_est=est
           if est < best_est - 1e-12:
               exc=exact_c5(candidate,n)
               if exc < best_c:
                   best_h=candidate.copy(); best_c=float(exc); best_est=float(est)
           # Even if candidate not best, maybe sym? no
       else:
           cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_est=best_est
    # After L-BFGS, maybe exact best_h
    return best_h,best_c,best_est
```
Potential issue: `hasattr(res_type,'x')` always; if minimize raises caught? We don't catch here; wrap in try. Add try around minimize.
```
       try: res=minimize(...)
       except Exception: res=None
```

def run_slsqp(h,n,deadline,eval_time=None,max_active=None):

    alphas=[200.0,1000.0,5000.0]
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_est=best_est
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time>deadline-3: break
       c=compute_c_lags(cur_h,n); active=select_active(c,max_active, rel_gap=0.003, abs_gap=5e-4)

       except: res=None
       if res...: candidate=project; est; update
    return best...
```

`run_pg`:
```
def run_pg(h,n,deadline,eval_time=None):
    h=project; best...
    if eval_time None estimate
    active_count=int(min(16,max(6,4+n//200)))
    alphas=[20.0,100.0,500.0,2000.0]
    cur_h=h; cur_est=best_est
    max_steps=int(min(200, max(20, (deadline-time.time()) / max(20*eval_time,1e-4))))
    step=0
    for alpha in alphas:
      for it in range(max_steps):
        if time>deadline-2: break
        c=compute_c_lags(cur_h,n); active=select_active(c,active_count)
        vals=c[active]; m=vals.max(); sh=alpha*(vals-m); sh-=sh.max(); w=exp; s=w.sum(); if s==0: w=sum=1 else w/=s
        g=active_grad(cur_h,n,active,w)
        g-=g.mean()
        norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
        if norm<1e-12: break
        g/=norm
        base_m=float(c.max())
        best_t=0.0; best_m=base_m
        # steps maybe keep previous best? use fixed list
        for t in (0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0004,0.0001):
           cand=project_feasible(cur_h + t*g,n)
           estv=compute_c_lags_max(cand,n)
           if estv < best_m - 1e-12:
              best_m=estv; best_t=t
        if best_t == 0.0:
           # try smaller or break
           break
        cand=project_feasible(cur_h + best_t*g,n)
        cur_h=cand; cur_est=best_m
        if cur_est < best_est - 1e-10:
           if (it % 10 == 0) or time>deadline:
              exc=exact_c5(cur_h,n)
              if exc<best_c: best_h=cur_h; best_c=exc; best_est=cur_est
      # after alpha maybe accept best_h if cur lower
    if cur_est < best_est: exact update
    return best...
```
This uses c full base_m. Step 0.02 on unit gradient may be too big? Projection handles. Could try larger first 0.1. But unit gradient scale depends; penalties. Use [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0004,0.0001]. Good.
If no step improves at alpha, break; maybe lower alpha continue.
Exact updates occasional; expensive. Instead after each accepted, if est improvement >1e-8, exact every 5. OK.

`improve_one`:

def improve_one(h,n,deadline):
    start=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    # maybe quick L-BFGS calibration
    eval_time=estimate_eval_time(h,n,10)
    reserve=int(min(90, max(15, 0.2*(deadline-time.start))))
    lbfgs_deadline=deadline-reserve
    h,bc,be=run_lbfgs(h,n,lbfgs_deadline,eval_time)
    # maybe sym update
    hs=project_feasible((h+h[::-1])*0.5,n)
    ests=compute_c_lags_max(hs,n)
    if ests < be - 1e-10: exact update
    if time.time()<deadline-20:
       h,bc,be=run_slsqp(h,n,deadline-20,eval_time)
    if time.time()<deadline-5:
       h,bc,be=run_pg(h,n,deadline-5,eval_time)
    # final sym
    hs=project...
    if exact hs lower update
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=exact_c5(h,n)
    return h,c
```

`upsample(h,n_new)`:
```
def upsample(h,n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    if n_old == n_new: return h.copy()
    if n_new % n_old ==0:
       return np.repeat(h, n_new//n_old)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
```
After upsample, project.
If n_new multiple, repeat may create discontinuities but okay. If ratio large 4. Good.

   h=bits.astype(float)

Binary helpers details:
```
def bit_reverse(i,k):
    r=0
    for _ in range(k):
       r=(r<<1)|(i&1)
       i >>=1
    return r

def initial_binary_ldc(n, sym=False):
    bits=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if sym and n %4 ==0:
       pair_count=n//2
       need=pair_count//2
       k=pair_count.bit_length()
       pairs=[]
       for p in range(pair_count): pairs.append((bit_reverse(p,k), p))
       pairs.sort()
       for _,p in pairs[:need]:
          bits[p]=1; bits[n-1-p]=1
       return bits
    target=n//2
    k=n.bit_length()
    pairs=[]
    for i in range(n): pairs.append((bit_reverse(i,k), i))
    pairs.sort()
    for _,i in pairs[:target]: bits[i]=1
    return bits
```
For n=500, bit_length 9? 500<512, k=9. Pair_count 250 bit_length 8? 250<256, k=8. Good.
If n not divisible by4 and sym True, fallback non-sym. Or choose n divisible by4 for binary. We'll set n_bin = n1 if n1%4==0 else nearest multiple? 500%4=0. 1000%4=0. Good.

`bits_to_B`: Could use `int.from_bytes` faster:
```
def bits_to_B(bits):
    B=0
    # process chunks maybe
    for i,b in enumerate(bits):
        if b: B |= (1 << i)
    return B
```
Fine.

`B_to_bits(B,n)`: loop.

`make_masks(n)`:
```
def make_masks(n):
    masks=[]
    for d in range(1,n):
       masks.append((1 << (n-d)) -1)
    return masks
```

`binary_max_count(B,n,masks)`:
```
def binary_max_count(B,n,masks):
    maxc=0
    for d, mask in enumerate(masks, start=1):
        Bm = B & mask
        s = (B >> d) & mask
        trans = (Bm ^ s).bit_count()
        if trans > maxc:
            t10 = (Bm & ~s).bit_count()
            if t10 > maxc: maxc=t10
            t01 = trans - t10
            if t01 > maxc: maxc=t01
    return maxc
```
`enumerate` with start okay. `mask` for d maybe if n-d=0? masks length n-1 d=1..n-1, n-d>=1. Good.
For Bm = B & mask: B may have bits beyond mask? no, but okay. `~s` negative, Bm & ~s works.

`initial_binary_from_h(h,n)`:
```
def initial_binary_from_h(h,n):
    bits=(h > 0.5).astype(np.int8)
    target=n//2
    ones_idx=np.flatnonzero(bits)
    zeros_idx=np.flatnonzero(1-bits)
    current=ones_idx.size
    if current < target:
       need=target-current
       if zeros_idx.size >= need:
          order=zeros_idx[np.argsort(-h[zeros_idx])[:need]]
          bits[order]=1
       # if not enough, set first zeros
       else: bits[zeros_idx]=1
    elif current > target:
       surplus=current-target
       if ones_idx.size >= surplus:
          order=ones_idx[np.argsort(h[ones_idx])[:surplus]]
          bits[order]=0
       else: bits[ones_idx]=0
    # if still mismatch due n odd? target floor? We choose even.
    # ensure exact target: if sum > target flip, etc.
    while bits.sum() > target:
       idx=np.argmin(bits + 0.0?) # need find one with bit1; use h smallest
       ones_idx=np.flatnonzero(bits); bits[ones_idx[np.argmin(h[ones_idx])]]=0
    while bits.sum() < target:
       zeros_idx=np.flatnonzero(1-bits); bits[zeros_idx[np.argmax(h[zeros_idx])]]=1
    return bits
```

```

Potential bugs in `select_active` using `np.argsort(c)[-min(...):]` when min > len. Use:
```
def select_active(c, max_active=10, rel_gap=0.002, abs_gap=2e-4):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    gap=max(abs_gap, rel_gap*m)
    cand=np.where(c >= m-gap)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
       cand=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if cand.size > max_active:
       order=np.argsort(c[cand])[::-1][:max_active]
       cand=cand[order]
    else:
       min_active=3
       if cand.size < min_active:
          min_active=min(min_active, len(c))
          top=np.argsort(c)[-min_active:]
          mask=np.zeros(len(c), dtype=bool)
          mask[cand]=True
          add=top[~mask[top]]
          room=max(0, max_active-cand.size)
          if add.size > room: add=add[:room]
          if add.size: cand=np.concatenate((cand, add))
    return cand.astype(int, copy=False)
```
If cand after >max? okay.

`def sum_cons_state_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(len(x), dtype=float)
    if len(x) > n:
        g[:n] = 1.0
    return g`
Length n+1; g[:n]=1.

`def active_ineq_jac(x, n, active):
    h = x[:n]
    K = len(active)
    J = np.zeros((K, n+1), dtype=float)
    if K == 0: return J
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    for i, idx in enumerate(active):
       add_lag_grad(J[i, :n], h, n, int(idx), dx)
       J[i, n] = -1.0
    return J`
`add_lag_grad` expects `g` array length n, x length n. Good.

`def c_obj(x,n): return float(x[-1])`
`def c_obj_jac(x,n): g=np.zeros(len(x)); g[-1]=1.0; return g`

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time
import math


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if y.shape[0] != n:
        y = np.resize(y, n)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)

    if n == 0:
        return y

    s = float(y.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
        diff = target - s
        if diff != 0.0:
            free = np.where((y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
            if free.size:
                y[free[0]] += diff
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
        return y

    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max())

    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        z = np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(z.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    y = np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 0:
        free = np.where((y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if free.size:
            y[free] += diff / float(free.size)
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - float(y.sum())
            if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
                y[free[0]] += diff
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            if diff > 0:
                idxs = np.where(y < 0.5)[0]
            else:
                idxs = np.where(y > 0.5)[0]
            if idxs.size:
                y[idxs[0]] += diff
            else:
                y[0] += diff
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 0:
        free = np.where((y > 1e-9) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-9))[0]
        if free.size:
            y[free[0]] += diff
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)

    return y


def compute_c_lags(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    total = float(h.sum())

    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])

    f = np.fft.rfft(h, 2 * n)
    q = np.fft.irfft(f * np.conjugate(f), 2 * n)[:n]

    c = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    mid = n - 1
    c[mid] = dx * (total - q[0])

    if n > 1:
        ee = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.intp)
        mm = n - ee
        c[mid + ee] = dx * (pref[mm] - q[ee])
        c[mid - ee] = dx * (total - pref[ee] - q[ee])

    return c


def compute_c_lags_max(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_c_lags(h, n)))


def exact_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def select_active(c, max_active=10, rel_gap=0.002, abs_gap=2e-4):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    gap = max(abs_gap, rel_gap * m)
    cand = np.where(c >= m - gap)[0]

    if cand.size == 0:
        cand = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

    if cand.size > max_active:
        order = np.argsort(c[cand])[::-1][:max_active]
        cand = cand[order]
    else:
        min_active = min(3, len(c))
        if cand.size < min_active:
            top = np.argsort(c)[-min_active:]
            mask = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=bool)
            mask[cand] = True
            add = top[~mask[top]]
            room = max(0, max_active - cand.size)
            if add.size > room:
                add = add[:room]
            if add.size:
                cand = np.concatenate((cand, add))

    return cand.astype(int, copy=False)


def add_lag_grad(g, x, n, idx, scale):
    mid = n - 1
    d = int(idx) - mid

    if d == 0:
        if scale != 0.0:
            g += scale * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
        return

    e = abs(d)
    m = n - e
    if m <= 0:
        return

    if d > 0:
        g[:m] += scale * (1.0 - x[e:n])
        if e != 0:
            g[e:] += scale * (-x[:m])
    else:
        g[e:] += scale * (1.0 - x[:m])
        if e != 0:
            g[:m] += scale * (-x[e:n])


def active_grad(x, n, active, weights):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    for idx, wt in zip(active, weights):
        add_lag_grad(g, x, n, idx, float(wt) * dx)
    return g


def soft_active_obj(x, n, active, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_c_lags(x, n)
    vals = c[active]
    m = float(vals.max())
    sh = alpha * (vals - m)
    sh = sh - sh.max()
    val = m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))) / alpha
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
        val += mu * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def soft_active_grad(x, n, active, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_c_lags(x, n)
    vals = c[active]
    m = float(vals.max())
    sh = alpha * (vals - m)
    sh = sh - sh.max()
    w = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(w)
        s = float(w.sum())
    w = w / s

    g = active_grad(x, n, active, w)
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
        g += 2.0 * mu * diff
    return g


def mean_active_obj(x, n, active, mu):
    c = compute_c_lags(x, n)
    vals = c[active]
    val = float(np.mean(vals))
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
        val += mu * diff * diff
    return val


def mean_active_grad(x, n, active, mu):
    k = max(1, len(active))
    w = np.full(k, 1.0 / float(k), dtype=float)
    g = active_grad(x, n, active, w)
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
        g += 2.0 * mu * diff
    return g


def sum_cons(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)


def sum_cons_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def sum_cons_state(x, n):
    return float(x[:n].sum() - 0.5 * n)


def sum_cons_state_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(len(x), dtype=float)
    g[:n] = 1.0
    return g


def c_obj(x, n):
    return float(x[-1])


def c_obj_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(len(x), dtype=float)
    g[-1] = 1.0
    return g


def active_ineq_fun(x, n, active):
    h = x[:n]
    C = float(x[-1])
    c = compute_c_lags(h, n)
    return c[active] - C


def active_ineq_jac(x, n, active):
    h = x[:n]
    k = len(active)
    if k == 0:
        return np.zeros((0, n + 1), dtype=float)
    J = np.zeros((k, n + 1), dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    for i, idx in enumerate(active):
        add_lag_grad(J[i, :n], h, n, int(idx), dx)
        J[i, n] = -1.0
    return J


def estimate_eval_time(h, n, active_count=10):
    active = np.arange(min(max(1, active_count), 2 * n - 1), dtype=int)
    reps = 3
    t = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(reps):
        soft_active_obj(h, n, active, 100.0, 0.0)
        soft_active_grad(h, n, active, 100.0, 0.0)
    dt = (time.perf_counter() - t) / float(reps)
    return max(1e-4, dt)


def run_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, eval_time=None, max_active=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    best_est = compute_c_lags_max(h, n)

    if eval_time is None:
        eval_time = estimate_eval_time(h, n, 10)

    if max_active is None:
        max_active = int(min(22, max(6, 4 + n // 180)))

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    mu = 1e-2
    alphas = [10.0, 60.0, 250.0, 1000.0, 4000.0, 15000.0]
    tasks = [("mean", None)] + [("soft", a) for a in alphas]

    cur_h = h.copy()
    cur_est = best_est

    for name, alpha in tasks:
        if time.time() > deadline - 3:
            break

        c = compute_c_lags(cur_h, n)
        active = select_active(c, max_active=max_active, rel_gap=0.002, abs_gap=2e-4)

        remaining = deadline - time.time()
        if remaining < 8:
            break

        slice_time = min(75.0, max(5.0, remaining * 0.35))
        maxiter = int(min(700, max(25, slice_time / max(8.0 * eval_time, 1e-4))))
        maxfun = max(100, 8 * maxiter)

        opts = {
            "maxiter": maxiter,
            "ftol": 1e-18,
            "gtol": 1e-12,
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxls": 30,
            "m": 20
        }

        try:
            if name == "mean":
                res = minimize(
                    mean_active_obj,
                    cur_h,
                    jac=mean_active_grad,
                    args=(n, active, mu),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options=opts
                )
            else:
                res = minimize(
                    soft_active_obj,
                    cur_h,
                    jac=soft_active_grad,
                    args=(n, active, alpha, mu),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options=opts
                )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            candidate = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            est = compute_c_lags_max(candidate, n)

            if est <= cur_est + 1e-10:
                cur_h = candidate
                cur_est = est

            if est < best_est - 1e-12:
                exc = exact_c5(candidate, n)
                if exc < best_c:
                    best_h = candidate.copy()
                    best_c = float(exc)
                    best_est = float(est)

    return best_h, best_c, best_est


def run_slsqp(h, n, deadline, eval_time=None, max_active=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    best_est = compute_c_lags_max(h, n)

    if eval_time is None:
        eval_time = estimate_eval_time(h, n, 8)

    if max_active is None:
        max_active = int(min(16, max(5, 3 + n // 250)))

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [
        {"type": "eq", "fun": sum_cons, "jac": sum_cons_jac, "args": (n,)}
    ]

    cur_h = h.copy()
    cur_est = best_est
    alphas = [300.0, 1500.0, 6000.0]

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 3:
            break

        c = compute_c_lags(cur_h, n)
        active = select_active(c, max_active=max_active, rel_gap=0.003, abs_gap=5e-4)

        remaining = deadline - time.time()
        if remaining < 15:
            break

        slice_time = min(40.0, remaining * 0.4)
        maxiter = int(min(800, max(60, slice_time / max(25.0 * eval_time, 1e-4))))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                soft_active_obj,
                cur_h,
                jac=soft_active_grad,
                args=(n, active, alpha, 0.0),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-14}
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            candidate = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            est = compute_c_lags_max(candidate, n)

            if est <= cur_est + 1e-10:
                cur_h = candidate
                cur_est = est

            if est < best_est - 1e-12:
                exc = exact_c5(candidate, n)
                if exc < best_c:
                    best_h = candidate.copy()
                    best_c = float(exc)
                    best_est = float(est)

    return best_h, best_c, best_est


def run_slsqp_c(h, n, deadline, eval_time=None, max_active=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    best_est = compute_c_lags_max(h, n)

    if eval_time is None:
        eval_time = estimate_eval_time(h, n, 8)

    if max_active is None:
        max_active = int(min(18, max(5, 3 + n // 200)))

    bounds_h = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    bounds_C = (0.05, 0.75)
    bounds = bounds_h + [bounds_C]

    cons_ground = [
        {"type": "eq", "fun": sum_cons_state, "jac": sum_cons_state_jac, "args": (n,)}
    ]

    cur_h = h.copy()
    C0 = min(0.75, max(0.05, best_c * 1.002 + 1e-4))

    for outer in range(3):
        if time.time() > deadline - 3:
            break

        c = compute_c_lags(cur_h, n)
        active = select_active(c, max_active=max_active, rel_gap=0.003, abs_gap=5e-4)

        cons = cons_ground + [
            {
                "type": "ineq",
                "fun": active_ineq_fun,
                "jac": active_ineq_jac,
                "args": (n, active)
            }
        ]

        x0 = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
        x0[:n] = cur_h
        x0[-1] = C0

        remaining = deadline - time.time()
        if remaining < 20:
            break

        slice_time = min(45.0, remaining * 0.35)
        maxiter = int(min(700, max(60, slice_time / max(30.0 * eval_time, 1e-4))))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                c_obj,
                x0,
                jac=c_obj_jac,
                args=(n,),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-13}
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            break

        candidate = project_feasible(res.x[:n], n)
        est = compute_c_lags_max(candidate, n)
        exc = exact_c5(candidate, n)

        C_opt = float(res.x[-1])
        if est < best_est - 1e-12 and exc < best_c:
            best_h = candidate.copy()
            best_c = float(exc)
            best_est = float(est)

        if est <= compute_c_lags_max(cur_h, n) + 1e-10:
            cur_h = candidate
        else:
            cur_h = best_h

        C0 = min(0.75, max(C_opt + 1e-5, best_c * 1.0005))

    return best_h, best_c, best_est


def run_pg(h, n, deadline, eval_time=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    best_est = compute_c_lags_max(h, n)

    if eval_time is None:
        eval_time = estimate_eval_time(h, n, 10)

    active_count = int(min(18, max(6, 4 + n // 160)))
    alphas = [20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0]

    cur_h = h.copy()
    cur_est = best_est

    max_steps = int(min(220, max(25, (deadline - time.time()) / max(25.0 * eval_time, 1e-4))))

    for alpha in alphas:
        for it in range(max_steps):
            if time.time() > deadline - 2:
                break

            c = compute_c_lags(cur_h, n)
            active = select_active(c, max_active=active_count, rel_gap=0.002, abs_gap=2e-4)

            vals = c[active]
            m = float(vals.max())
            sh = alpha * (vals - m)
            sh = sh - sh.max()
            w = np.exp(sh)
            s = float(w.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                w = np.ones_like(w)
                s = float(w.sum())
            w = w / s

            g = active_grad(cur_h, n, active, w)
            g -= g.mean()
            norm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if norm < 1e-12:
                break
            g /= norm

            base_m = float(c.max())
            best_t = 0.0
            best_m = base_m

            for t in (0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0004, 0.0001):
                cand = project_feasible(cur_h + t * g, n)
                estv = compute_c_lags_max(cand, n)
                if estv < best_m - 1e-13:
                    best_m = estv
                    best_t = t

            if best_t <= 0.0:
                break

            cur_h = project_feasible(cur_h + best_t * g, n)
            cur_est = best_m

            if cur_est < best_est - 1e-10 and (it % 10 == 0 or time.time() > deadline - 10):
                exc = exact_c5(cur_h, n)
                if exc < best_c:
                    best_h = cur_h.copy()
                    best_c = float(exc)
                    best_est = float(cur_est)

        if cur_est < best_est - 1e-11:
            exc = exact_c5(cur_h, n)
            if exc < best_c:
                best_h = cur_h.copy()
                best_c = float(exc)
                best_est = float(cur_est)

    return best_h, best_c, best_est


def improve_one(h, n, deadline):
    start = time.time()
    h = project_feasible(h, n)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5), n)

    eval_time = estimate_eval_time(h, n, min(12, max(4, n // 100)))

    allocated = max(1.0, deadline - start)
    reserve = min(90.0, max(15.0, 0.22 * allocated))
    lbfgs_deadline = deadline - reserve

    h, bc, be = run_lbfgs(h, n, lbfgs_deadline, eval_time=eval_time)

    hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (h + h[::-1]), n)
    ests = compute_c_lags_max(hs, n)
    if ests < be - 1e-11:
        excs = exact_c5(hs, n)
        if excs < bc:
            h = hs.copy()
            bc = float(excs)
            be = float(ests)

    if n <= 900 and time.time() < deadline - 25:
        h, bc, be = run_slsqp_c(h, n, deadline - 25, eval_time=eval_time)

    if n <= 900 and time.time() < deadline - 15:
        h, bc, be = run_slsqp(h, n, deadline - 15, eval_time=eval_time)

    if time.time() < deadline - 4:
        h, bc, be = run_pg(h, n, deadline - 4, eval_time=eval_time)

    hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (h + h[::-1]), n)
    ests = compute_c_lags_max(hs, n)
    if ests < be - 1e-11:
        excs = exact_c5(hs, n)
        if excs < bc:
            h = hs.copy()
            bc = float(excs)
            be = float(ests)

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    bc = exact_c5(h, n)
    return h.copy(), float(bc)


def upsample(h, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return h.copy()

    if n_old > 0 and n_new > 0 and n_new % n_old == 0:
        return np.repeat(h, n_new // n_old)

    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)


def bit_reverse(i, k):
    r = 0
    x = int(i)
    for _ in range(k):
        r = (r << 1) | (x & 1)
        x >>= 1
    return r


def initial_binary_ldc(n, sym=False):
    bits = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if n <= 0:
        return bits

    if sym and n % 4 == 0:
        pair_count = n // 2
        need = pair_count // 2
        if need > 0:
            k = max(1, pair_count.bit_length())
            pairs = []
            for p in range(pair_count):
                pairs.append((bit_reverse(p, k), p))
            pairs.sort()
            for _, p in pairs[:need]:
                bits[p] = 1
                bits[n - 1 - p] = 1
            return bits

    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0:
        return bits
    k = max(1, n.bit_length())
    pairs = []
    for i in range(n):
        pairs.append((bit_reverse(i, k), i))
    pairs.sort()
    for _, i in pairs[:target]:
        bits[i] = 1
    return bits


def make_initial_fallback(n):
    bits = initial_binary_ldc(max(2, int(n)), sym=False)
    h = bits.astype(float)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def bits_to_B(bits):
    B = 0
    for i, b in enumerate(bits):
        if b:
            B |= (1 << i)
    return B


def B_to_bits(B, n):
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    for i in range(n):
        out[i] = 1 if ((B >> i) & 1) else 0
    return out


def make_masks(n):
    masks = []
    for d in range(1, n):
        masks.append((1 << (n - d)) - 1)
    return masks


def binary_max_count(B, n, masks):
    maxc = 0
    for d, mask in enumerate(masks, start=1):
        rem = n - d
        if rem <= maxc:
            break
        Bm = B & mask
        s = (B >> d) & mask
        trans = (Bm ^ s).bit_count()
        if trans > maxc:
            t10 = (Bm & ~s).bit_count()
            if t10 > maxc:
                maxc = t10
            t01 = trans - t10
            if t01 > maxc:
                maxc = t01
    return int(maxc)


def initial_binary_from_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    bits = (h > 0.5).astype(np.int8)
    target = n // 2

    ones_idx = np.flatnonzero(bits)
    zeros_idx = np.flatnonzero(1 - bits)

    current = int(ones_idx.size)
    if current < target:
        need = target - current
        if zeros_idx.size >= need:
            order = zeros_idx[np.argsort(-h[zeros_idx])[:need]]
            bits[order] = 1
        else:
            bits[zeros_idx] = 1
    elif current > target:
        surplus = current - target
        if ones_idx.size >= surplus:
            order = ones_idx[np.argsort(h[ones_idx])[:surplus]]
            bits[order] = 0
        else:
            bits[ones_idx] = 0

    for _ in range(n + 5):
        s = int(bits.sum())
        if s == target:
            break
        elif s > target:
            ones_idx = np.flatnonzero(bits)
            if ones_idx.size == 0:
                break
            j = ones_idx[np.argmin(h[ones_idx])]
            bits[j] = 0
        else:
            zeros_idx = np.flatnonzero(1 - bits)
            if zeros_idx.size == 0:
                break
            j = zeros_idx[np.argmax(h[zeros_idx])]
            bits[j] = 1

    return bits


def binary_sa(bits_input, n, deadline, seed=123):
    if n <= 0 or int(bits_input.sum()) != n // 2:
        return bits_input.copy()

    bits = bits_input.astype(np.int8).copy()
    B = bits_to_B(bits)
    masks = make_masks(n)
    bit_masks = [1 << i for i in range(n)]

    curr = binary_max_count(B, n, masks)
    best_B = B
    best_count = curr

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + int(n) * 1000003)
    T = 10.0
    min_T = 0.30
    cool = 0.999
    no_improve = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        a = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        b = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        if a == b:
            continue
        if bits[a] == bits[b]:
            continue

        B2 = B ^ bit_masks[a] ^ bit_masks[b]
        cnt2 = binary_max_count(B2, n, masks)

        accept = False
        if cnt2 < curr:
            accept = True
        else:
            delta = (curr - cnt2) / T
            if delta > -50.0 and rng.random() < math.exp(delta):
                accept = True

        if accept:
            B = B2
            bits[a] = 1 if bits[a] == 0 else 0
            bits[b] = 1 if bits[b] == 0 else 0
            curr = cnt2

            if cnt2 < best_count:
                best_count = cnt2
                best_B = B2
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if T > min_T:
            T *= cool
        else:
            if no_improve > 900:
                B = best_B
                bits = B_to_bits(B, n)
                curr = best_count
                T = 5.0
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                T = min_T

    return B_to_bits(best_B, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 3.0

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or len(parent) == 0:
        n1 = 500
        h1 = make_initial_fallback(n1)
    else:
        ip = len(parent)
        if ip < 300:
            n1 = 500
        elif ip > 1000:
            n1 = 1000
        else:
            n1 = ip
        if n1 % 2 != 0:
            n1 += 1
        h1 = upsample(parent, n1)

    h1 = project_feasible(h1, n1)
    c1 = exact_c5(h1, n1)

    best_h = h1.copy()
    best_c = float(c1)
    best_n = int(n1)

    hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (h1 + h1[::-1]), n1)
    cs = exact_c5(hs, n1)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h = hs.copy()
        best_c = float(cs)
        best_n = int(n1)

    if time.time() > deadline - 5:
        return project_feasible(best_h, best_n), float(exact_c5(best_h, best_n)), int(best_n)

    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    allocated1 = min(260.0, max(30.0, remaining * 0.28))
    dl1 = time.time() + allocated1
    if dl1 > deadline - 120:
        dl1 = deadline - 120

    h1, c1 = improve_one(best_h, best_n, dl1)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_h = h1.copy()
        best_c = float(c1)
        best_n = int(best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 450:
        n_bin = 500
        h_bin_start = upsample(best_h, n_bin)
        h_bin_start = project_feasible(h_bin_start, n_bin)
        bits0 = initial_binary_from_h(h_bin_start, n_bin)

        dl_sa = time.time() + min(28.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.05)
        bits_best = binary_sa(bits0, n_bin, dl_sa, seed=seed)
        h_bin = project_feasible(bits_best.astype(float), n_bin)
        cb = exact_c5(h_bin, n_bin)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h = h_bin.copy()
            best_c = float(cb)
            best_n = int(n_bin)

        if time.time() < deadline - 420:
            allocated_bin = min(130.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.12)
            dl_bin = time.time() + allocated_bin
            if dl_bin > deadline - 260:
                dl_bin = deadline - 260
            h_bin, c_bin = improve_one(h_bin, n_bin, dl_bin)
            if c_bin < best_c:
                best_h = h_bin.copy()
                best_c = float(c_bin)
                best_n = int(n_bin)

    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining > 320:
        n2 = 1000 if best_n < 1000 else best_n
        if n2 != best_n:
            h2 = upsample(best_h, n2)
        else:
            h2 = best_h.copy()
        h2 = project_feasible(h2, n2)

        allocated2 = min(430.0, remaining * 0.42)
        dl2 = time.time() + allocated2
        if dl2 > deadline - 160:
            dl2 = deadline - 160

        h2, c2 = improve_one(h2, n2, dl2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = float(c2)
            best_n = int(n2)

    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining > 220 and best_n < 1500:
        n3 = 1500
        h3 = upsample(best_h, n3)
        h3 = project_feasible(h3, n3)

        allocated3 = min(210.0, remaining * 0.30)
        dl3 = time.time() + allocated3
        if dl3 > deadline - 60:
            dl3 = deadline - 60

        h3, c3 = improve_one(h3, n3, dl3)
        if c3 < best_c:
            best_h = h3.copy()
            best_c = float(c3)
            best_n = int(n3)

    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining > 45:
        final_deadline = deadline - 2
        h_final, c_final = improve_one(best_h, best_n, final_deadline)
        if c_final < best_c:
            best_h = h_final.copy()
            best_c = float(c_final)
            best_n = int(best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_h)):
        n_fallback = 500
        best_h = make_initial_fallback(n_fallback)
        best_n = int(n_fallback)
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)
    else:
        best_c = float(exact_c5(best_h, best_n))

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
